# Kanto postgame teams

All teams contain six Pokemon and differ across all four modes. Levels are
90-92 Easy, 94-96 Normal, 97-99 Hard and 100 Kaizo. Brisk mode rules supply
AI, prices, bag rules and EXP behavior; these parties own their actual sets.
